<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Ubayy Ibn Ka&#x27;b reported: I arked the Messenger of Allah (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) about a man who has sexual intercourse with his wife, but leaves her before orgasm. Upon this he (the Holy Prophet) said: He should wash the secretion of his wife, and then perform ablution and ofier prayer</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sahih Muslim · 779]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/779" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sahih Muslim   779 ]</p>

</details>

<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">हम्माद और अबू मुआविया ने हिशाम बिन उरवा से, उन्होंने अपने वालिद से, उन्होंने अबू अय्यूब से, उन्होंने हज़रत उबय्य बिन का&#x27;अब (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) से रिवायत की, कहा: मैं ने रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) से उस मर्द के बारे में पूछा जो अपनी बीवी के पास जाता है, फिर उसे इनज़ाला (डिस्चार्ज) नहीं होता। तो आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया: बीवी से उसे जो कुछ लग जाए उसे धो डाले, फिर वुज़ू कर के नमाज़ पढ़ ले।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sahih Muslim · 779]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/779" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حماد اور ابو معاویہ نےہشام بن عروہ سے ، انہوں نے اپنےوالد سے ، انہوں نے ابو ایوب سے ، انہوں نے حضرت ابی بن کعب ‌رضی ‌اللہ ‌عنہ ‌ ‌ سے روایت کی ، کہا : میں رسول اللہ ﷺ سے اس مرد کے بارے میں پوچھا جو اپنی بیوی کے پاس جاتا ہے ، پھر اسے انزال نہیں ہوتا ۔ تو آپ نے فرمایا : ’’ بیوی سے اسے جو کچھ لگ جائے اس کو دھو ڈالے ، پھر وضو کر کے نماز پڑھ لے ۔ ‘ ‘</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Muslim · 779]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/779" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Hammad aur Abu Muawiyah ne Hisham bin Urwah se, unhone apne walid se, unhone Abu Ayyub se, unhone Hazrat Ubayy bin Ka&#x27;ab (Radi Allahu Anhu) se riwayat ki, kaha: Main ne Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) se us mard ke bare mein pucha jo apni biwi ke paas jata hai, phir use inzala (discharge) nahi hota. To Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya: Biwi se use jo kuch lag jaye use dho dale, phir wuzu kar ke namaz parh le.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sahih Muslim · 779]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/779" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 750

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-muslim%3A780)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 3</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنَا مُحَمَّدُ بْنُ الْمُثَنَّى، حَدَّثَنَا مُحَمَّدُ بْنُ جَعْفَرٍ، حَدَّثَنَا شُعْبَةُ، عَنْ هِشَامِ بْنِ عُرْوَةَ، حَدَّثَنِي أَبِي، عَنِ الْمَلِيِّ، عَنِ الْمَلِيِّ، - يَعْنِي بِقَوْلِهِ الْمَلِيِّ عَنِ الْمَلِيِّ أَبُو أَيُّوبَ، - عَنْ أُبَىِّ بْنِ كَعْبٍ، عَنْ رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم أَنَّهُ قَالَ فِي الرَّجُلِ يَأْتِي أَهْلَهُ ثُمَّ لاَ يُنْزِلُ قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ يَغْسِلُ ذَكَرَهُ وَيَتَوَضَّأُ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Muslim · 780]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/780" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sahih Muslim · 780</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sahih Muslim   780 ]</p>

</details>

<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Ubayy ibn Ka&#x27;b narrated it from the Messenger of Allah (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) that he said: If a person has sexual intercourse with his wife, but does not experience orgasm, he should wash his organ and perform an ablution</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sahih Muslim · 780]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/780" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">शु&#x27;बा ने हिशाम बिन उरवह से बाक़ी मन्दा सबका सनद के साथ हज़रात उबय्य बिन का&#x27;अब (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) से रिवायत की उन्होंने रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) से रिवायत की कि आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने उस मर्द के बारे में जो अपनी बीवी के पास जाता है फिर उसे इंज़ाला नहीं होता</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sahih Muslim · 780]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/780" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">شعبہ نے ہشام بن عروہ سے باقی ماندہ سابقہ سند کےساتھ حضرت ابی بن کعب ‌رضی ‌اللہ ‌عنہ ‌ ‌ سےروایت کی ، انہوں نے رسول اللہ ﷺسے روایت کی کہ آپ نے اس مرد کے بارے میں جواپنی بیوی کے پاس جاتا ہے پھر اسے انزال نہیں ہوتا ، فرمایا : ’’ وہ اپنے عضو کو دھو لے اور وضو کرے ۔ ‘ ‘</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Muslim · 780]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/780" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Shu&#x27;ba ne Hisham bin Urwah se baqi manda sabqa sanad ke sath Hazrat Ubayy bin Ka&#x27;ab (Radi Allahu Anhu) se riwayat ki, unhone Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) se riwayat ki ke Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne us mard ke bare mein jo apni biwi ke paas jata hai phir use inzala nahi hota, farmaya: Woh apne uzu ko dho le aur wuzu kare.</p>
